lxml uses None for default namespaces; section files use hp prefix in this task.\n    ns = {}\n    for k, v in (root.nsmap or {}).items():\n        if k is None:\n            continue\n        ns[k] = v\n    if 'hp' not in ns:\n        # Fallback for safety\n        ns['hp'] = 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n    return ns\n\n\ndef paragraph_text(p, ns):\n    # Concatenate all hp:t descendants\n    parts = []\n    for t in p.xpath('.//hp:t', namespaces=ns):\n        parts.append(t.text or '')\n    return ''.join(parts)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text_single_run(p, ns, new_text: str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replace paragraph text by setting first hp:t to new_text and clearing the rest.\n    Keeps structure largely intact but ensures no duplicate remnants remain.\n    \"\"\"\n    t_nodes = p.xpath('.//hp:t', namespaces=ns)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        # If no text nodes exist, create a minimal run/t node.\n        run = etree.SubElement(p, f\"{{{ns['hp']}}}run\")\n        run.set('charPrIDRef', '0')\n        t = etree.SubElement(run, f\"{{{ns['hp']}}}t\")\n        t.text = new_text\n        return\n\n    t_nodes[0].text = new_text\n    for t in t_nodes[1:]:\n        t.text = ''\n\n\ndef replace_text_across_runs(p, ns, old: str, new: str):\n    \"\"\"Replace substring in each hp:t node independently.\"\"\"\n    changed = False\n    for t in p.xpath('.//hp:t', namespaces=ns):\n        if t.text and old in t.text:\n            t.text = t.text.replace(old, new)\n            changed = True\n    return changed\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache(p, ns):\n    # Task mentions <hp:lineSegArray> but observed XML uses <hp:linesegarray>\n    # Remove either capitalization and nested lineseg elements.\n    removed = False\n    for tag in ['linesegarray', 'lineSegArray', 'lineseg', 'lineSeg']:\n        nodes = p.xpath(f'./hp:{tag}', namespaces=ns)\n        for n in nodes:\n            p.remove(n)\n            removed = True\n    # Also remove any descendant cache elements just in case\n    for tag in ['linesegarray', 'lineSegArray', 'lineseg', 'lineSeg']:\n        nodes = p.xpath(f'.//hp:{tag}', namespaces=ns)\n        for n in nodes:\n            parent = n.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(n)\n                removed = True\n    return removed\n\n\ndef is_appendix_paragraph(p, ns):\n    return '이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.' in paragraph_text(p, ns)\n\n\ndef update_section0(tree, updates):\n    ns = get_nsmap(tree)\n\n    # Build exact old->new mapping based on observed current document text.\n    # Customer is split across runs: \"Northwind \" + \"Retail\".\n    mappings = {\n        'Northwind Retail': updates['customer'],\n        '이전담당자': updates['owner_name'],\n        '2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15': f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\",\n        'Standard-18M': updates['band'],\n        'ops-old@northwind.example': updates['escalation_email'],\n        '기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.': updates['note'],\n    }\n\n    modified_paragraphs = set()\n\n    # Update paragraphs\n    for p in tree.xpath('.//hp:p', namespaces=ns):\n        if is_appendix_paragraph(p, ns):\n            continue\n\n        full = paragraph_text(p, ns)\n\n        # Special handling: customer line is split into two runs.\n        if '고객사:' in full and 'Northwind' in full and 'Retail' in full:\n            # Replace from \"고객사:\" onward to keep prefix exactly.\n            # Expected current: \"고객사: Northwind Retail\"\n            prefix = '고객사: '\n            if prefix in full:\n                new_full = prefix + updates['customer']\n                set_paragraph_text_single_run(p, ns, new_full)\n                modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n                continue\n\n        # Renewal window paragraph\n        if '갱신 윈도우:' in full and '2025-04-01' in full:\n            old_window = '2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15'\n            new_window = f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n            if replace_text_across_runs(p, ns, old_window, new_window):\n                modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n            continue\n\n        # For other fields, do substring replacement within runs.\n        changed_here = False\n        for old, new in mappings.items():\n            if old in full:\n                # When the old value spans runs, fall back to full reset.\n                if old == 'Northwind Retail':\n                    continue\n                if replace_text_across_runs(p, ns, old, new):\n                    changed_here = True\n        if changed_here:\n            modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n\n    # Remove layout cache for modified paragraphs\n    for p in modified_paragraphs:\n        remove_layout_cache(p, ns)\n\n    return len(modified_paragraphs)\n\n\ndef update_section1(tree, updates, followups):\n    ns = get_nsmap(tree)\n    modified_paragraphs = set()\n\n    #
